# Cat Prompts — Catalog - Epic

140 cats. Generated from the catalog by `tools/build_cat_prompts.py`; edit the script, not this page. Send the **icon prompt with the icon style reference** and the **3D prompt with the in-game 3D style reference** attached (both in `design/reference/user-style/`; see [[Assets/Cat Art Pipeline Plan]]).

## CE0751 — Spotted Domestic Shorthair
Epic • Domestic Shorthair • archetype A1 (Classic Shorthair)

**Icon prompt**

```text
[Attach the icon style reference.] Create a game icon of one cat. Style: match the attached icon style reference (design/reference/user-style/icon-style-reference.jpg) for style only, do not copy any cat from it. 2D cartoon game icon: chibi cat sitting upright, body facing front with the head turned very slightly toward the viewer, big round head about half of the height, small body with the front paws together, the tail curled around one side of the body. Clean dark outline (deep brown or navy, never pure black) of even weight around the whole cat and its main shapes; soft cel shading with two or three tones and a gentle gradient; small fluffy tufts on the cheeks and a little chest fluff; big glossy eyes with one white highlight, pink inner ears, small nose and mouth. Cute, friendly, consistent with a set of collectible pet icons. Subject: "Spotted Domestic Shorthair" (Epic). Body type: Classic Shorthair (balanced medium body, rounded head, medium triangular ears, medium tail; adapted to the style above). Family traits: Domestic Shorthair: plain everyday cat, short sleek coat. Coat and markings: Warm tan body; dark spotted coat. Eyes: hazel eyes. Accessories: none. Rarity is shown by the game UI frame, not by the cat: no glow, sparkles, gold trim, aura, crown or rarity colour unless listed under accessories. Framing: the whole cat centred with about 10% padding, transparent background, no ground shadow, no text, no card or frame. 1024x1024 PNG; it must still read clearly at 96 px. Avoid: realistic photo fur, extra limbs or toes, human features, clothing (unless listed), text, logos, watermarks, background scenery.
```

**3D model prompt**

```text
[Attach the in-game 3D style reference and, if approved, this cat's icon for its colours.] Create a 3D model of one cat. Style: match the attached in-game 3D style reference (design/reference/user-style/ingame-3d-style-reference.jpg) for style only, do not copy any cat from it. Low-poly faceted 3D cat with real cat proportions (not chibi): slim body, normal-sized head, long legs, tail raised high in a gentle curve. Flat-shaded polygons so the facets read, simple painted colour regions per face (no photo fur), small low-poly ears with pink inner faces, almond eyes, thin white whiskers. Natural, calm standing pose. Subject: "Spotted Domestic Shorthair" (Epic). Body type: Classic Shorthair (balanced medium body, rounded head, medium triangular ears, medium tail; adapted to the style above). Family traits: Domestic Shorthair: plain everyday cat, short sleek coat. Coat and markings: Warm tan body; dark spotted coat. Eyes: hazel eyes. Accessories: none. Rarity is shown by the game UI frame, not by the cat: no glow, sparkles, gold trim, aura, crown or rarity colour unless listed under accessories. 3D: game-ready for Roblox. 1,500-3,000 triangles, flat shading, one material, a small base-colour texture (512x512 is enough; colour regions may also be vertex colours), eyes and whiskers as separate thin geometry. Pose: standing on all fours with the tail up (rig-ready); origin centred under the feet; facing -Z; about 4 studs long. Deliver .blend source, FBX export, and front, side and three-quarter renders. Avoid: realistic photo fur, extra limbs or toes, human features, clothing (unless listed), text, logos, watermarks, background scenery.
```
